Method: 采用"模式优先于词根"的反转闪米特语模型，将屈折描述与派生、语义分离；通过元音数量（v/vv）而非质量来简化单数模式分类；将词根交替和拼写变异独立于模式编码；对破裂复数进行层次化分类（三字母：22模式×90类；四字母：3模式×70类），考虑单数屈折变化后扩展为300个屈折类；采用直接查词典的形态分析方式，无需中间规则。

Result: 建立了包含22个模式（90个子类）的三字母破裂复数分类和3个模式（70个子类）的四字母破裂复数分类，统一后得到300个屈折类，并对3200个破裂复数名词条目进行了编码实现。

Conclusion: 该模型相比传统阿拉伯语形态学更简洁有序，通过反转词根-词模关系并分离屈折与派生，为阿拉伯语名词形态描述和词典管理提供了一种实用且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: We present a substantially implemented model of description of the inflectional morphology of Arabic nouns, with special attention to the management of dictionaries and other language resources by Arabic-speaking linguists. The breakthrough lies in the reversal of the traditional root-and-pattern Semitic model into pattern-and-root, giving precedence to patterns over roots. Our model includes broken plurals (BPs), i.e. plurals formed by modifying the stem. It is based on the traditional notions of root and pattern of Semitic morphology. However, as compared to traditional Arabic morphology, it keeps the formal description of inflection separate from that of derivation and semantics. As traditional Arabic dictionaries, the updatable dictionary is structured in lexical entries for lemmas, and the reference spelling is fully diacritized. In our model, morphological analysis of Arabic text is performed directly with a dictionary of words and without morphophonological rules. Our taxonomy for noun inflection is simple, orderly and detailed. We simplify the taxonomy of singular patterns by specifying vowel quantity as v or vv, and ignoring vowel quality. Root alternations and orthographical variations are encoded independently from patterns and in a factual way, without deep roots or morphophonological or orthographical rules. Nouns with a triliteral BP are classified according to 22 patterns subdivided into 90 classes, and nouns with a quadriliteral BP according to 3 patterns subdivided into 70 classes. These 160 classes become 300 inflectional classes when we take into account inflectional variations that affect only the singular. We provide a straightforward encoding scheme that we applied to 3 200 entries of BP nouns.

</details>


### [146] [TransitLM: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Map-Free Transit Route Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22355)
*Hanyu Guo,Jiedong Yang,Chao Chen,Longfei Xu,Kaikui Liu,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TransitLM，一个包含超过1300万条公交路线规划记录的大规模数据集，覆盖中国四个城市的120,845个站点和13,666条线路。实验表明，在该数据集上训练的语言模型能够高精度生成结构有效的路线，并能隐式地将任意GPS坐标定位到合适的站点，实现无需地图的端到端路线生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公交路线规划依赖于结构化的地图基础设施和复杂的路由引擎，现有数据集无法支持训练模型绕过这种依赖，因此需要构建一个大规模数据集来实现数据驱动的路线规划。

Method: 构建了TransitLM数据集（1300万+记录，覆盖中国四城市），作为持续预训练语料和基准数据；设计了三个评估任务及互补指标；训练LLM进行端到端的路线生成。

Result: 训练后的LLM能高精度生成结构有效的路线，并能隐式地将任意GPS坐标定位到合适站点，无需显式地图映射。

Conclusion: 公交路线规划可以完全从数据中学习，实现了直接从起终点信息生成路线的端到端、无地图方法。

Abstract: Public transit route planning traditionally depends on structured map infrastructure and complex routing engines, and no existing dataset supports training models to bypass this dependency. We present TransitLM, a large-scale dataset of over 13 million transit route planning records from four Chinese cities covering 120,845 stations and 13,666 lines, released as a continual pre-training corpus and benchmark data for three evaluation tasks with complementary metrics. Experiments show that an LLM trained on TransitLM produces structurally valid routes at high accuracy and implicitly grounds arbitrary GPS coordinates to appropriate stations without any explicit mapping. These results demonstrate that transit route planning can be learned entirely from data, enabling end-to-end, map-free route generation directly from origin-destination information. The dataset and benchmark are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/GD-ML/TransitLM, with evaluation code at https://github.com/HotTricker/TransitLM.

</details>


### [147] [Modeling Pathology-Like Behavioral Patterns in Language Models Through Behavioral Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22356)
*Nicola Milano,Davide Marocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出行为归纳框架，通过在结构化决策任务上微调LLM（使用模拟抑郁和偏执等适应不良行为模式的合成数据集），使模型在多样上下文中一致选择特定行为类别。微调后的模型在开放语言任务中展现出稳定的、跨上下文的负向和威胁相关解释偏好，且该效应可泛化到训练情境之外。不同行为模式的诱导产生可分离的响应倾向，表明结构化行为训练产生有差异的策略级偏差而非通用分布扭曲。研究支持将LLM视为基于策略的系统，其中行为约束塑造 emergent 表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以通过行为优化（即微调模型使其在决策任务中一致选择特定行为模式）来系统性地改变LLM的生成分布，从而将行为层面的约束与语言生成联系起来，验证LLM作为策略系统的理论观点。

Method: 采用行为归纳框架：1) 构建合成数据集，模拟抑郁和偏执等适应不良行为模式；2) 在结构化决策任务上微调transformer语言模型；3) 通过开放语言任务的定性补全、心理测量式评估和Jensen-Shannon散度等定量指标检测生成分布的变化。

Result: 微调模型在下一词概率分布上出现稳定的、跨上下文的变化，包括负向和威胁相关解释的概率增加。该效应泛化到训练情境之外。不同行为模式的诱导产生可分离的响应倾向，而非通用分布扭曲。

Conclusion: 一致的行为优化可以在LLM中产生稳定的行为与分布模式，这些模式与改变后的潜在先验一致，将行动选择与语言生成联系起来。LLM可被视为基于策略的系统，其中行为约束塑造 emergent 表征结构，可作为计算认知模型中研究行为、解释与生成语言关系的受控测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as computational tools for modeling human-like behavior. We introduce a behavioral induction framework that modifies model policies through fine-tuning on structured decision-making tasks: using synthetic datasets inspired by maladaptive behavioral patterns, including depression and paranoia, we train transformer-based language models to consistently select specific classes of actions across diverse contexts. We then test whether this behavioral optimization produces systematic changes in generative distributions.
  Across two architectures, fine-tuned models show stable, context-general shifts in next-token probability distributions, including increased probability assigned to negative and threat-related interpretations in open-ended language tasks. These effects generalize beyond training contexts and are detectable in qualitative completions, psychometric-style evaluations, and quantitative distributional metrics such as Jensen-Shannon divergence.
  Induced behavioral profiles also show partial specificity. Models optimized for different behavioral patterns exhibit dissociable response tendencies across evaluation probes, suggesting that structured behavioral training produces differentiated policy-level biases rather than generic distributional skew.
  We interpret these findings as evidence that consistent behavioral optimization in LLMs can generate stable behavioral and distributional patterns consistent with altered latent priors, linking action selection and language generation. More broadly, the results support a view of LLMs as policy-based systems in which behavioral constraints shape emergent representational structure, highlighting their potential as controlled testbeds for studying the relationship between behavior, interpretation, and generative language in computational models of cognition.

</details>


### [148] [Multi-Stage Training for Abusive Comment Detection in Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22380)
*Pranshu Rastogi,Madhav Mathur,Ramaneswaran S,Kshitij Mohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言预处理和集成模型的方法来检测社交媒体上的 abusive 评论，通过最小化误报率来平衡内容检测与言论自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上 abusive 内容日益增多，需要有效的检测手段，同时要避免过度检测损害言论自由。

Method: 使用基于语言的预处理技术，结合多个模型的集成方法，通过大量实验优化管道以最小化误报率。

Result: 提出了一套能够有效降低将非 abusive 内容误判为 abusive 的检测管道。

Conclusion: 该管道能在检测 abusive 评论的同时不损害言论自由。

Abstract: In recent years social media has become an increasingly popular tool for communication. People use it to share their ideas, exchange information, and discuss thoughts. Given its prevalence and widespread reach, social media must remain a safe space for people. Content generated on social media can be abusive and it has become increasingly important to detect such content. In this paper, we use a language-based preprocessing and an ensemble of several models and analyze their performance of abusive comment detection. Through extensive experimentation, we propose a pipeline that minimizes the false-positive rate (marking non-abusive as abusive) so that these systems can detect abusive comments without undermining the freedom of expression.

</details>


### [149] [Unified Data Selection for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22389)
*Xiaoyuan Li,Yubo Ma,Chengpeng Li,Fengbin Zhu,Yiyao Yu,Keqin Bao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出高熵和（HES）指标，通过仅对推理样本中最高熵（如前0.5%）的token熵值求和来量化推理质量，该指标无需训练即可在SFT、RFT和RL三种训练范式下有效筛选高质量推理数据，显著降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练LLM进行复杂长链推理的方法要么计算成本高，要么无法可靠区分高质量与低质量的推理样本，亟需一个高效且有效的推理质量度量指标。

Method: 提出High-Entropy Sum (HES)指标，对每个推理样本中熵值最高的前0.5%token的熵值求和，作为推理质量的度量。在SFT、RFT和RL三种训练范式下验证其有效性。

Result: SFT中，用HES排名前20%的数据训练即可达到全数据集性能；RFT中HES方法显著优于基线；RL中HES筛选的成功轨迹使模型学习到强推理模式，显著超越其他对比方法。

Conclusion: HES是一种鲁棒、无需训练的统一度量标准，能够高效、有效地为LLM开发高级推理能力提供数据筛选方法。

Abstract: Effectively training Large Language Models (LLMs) for complex, long-CoT reasoning is often bottlenecked by the need for massive high-quality reasoning data. Existing methods are either computationally expensive or fail to reliably distinguish high- from low-quality reasoning samples. To address this, we propose High-Entropy Sum (HES), a training-free metric that quantifies reasoning quality by summing only the entropy of the top (e.g., 0.5\%) highest-entropy tokens in each reasoning sample. We validate HES across three mainstream training paradigms: Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT), Rejection Fine-tuning (RFT), and Reinforcement Learning (RL), with extensive results demonstrating its consistent effectiveness and significantly reduced computational overhead. In SFT, training on the top 20\% HES-ranked data matches full-dataset performance, while using the lowest-HES data degrades it. In RFT, our HES-based training approach significantly outperforms baseline methods. In RL, HES-selected successful trajectories enable the model to learn strong reasoning patterns, significantly surpassing other compared methods. Our findings establish HES as a robust, training-free metric that enables a unified, effective, and efficient method for developing advanced reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


### [150] [DeferMem: Query-Time Evidence Distillation via Reinforcement Learning for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22411)
*Jianing Yin,Tan Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeferMem提出了一种将长期记忆问答解耦为高召回候选检索和查询条件证据蒸馏的框架，使用轻量级段链接结构组织历史并检索候选，通过基于强化学习（DistillPO）的蒸馏器将噪声候选转化为忠实、自包含、查询相关的证据，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上实现了最佳QA准确率、最快运行时间和零API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在查询之前处理记忆，基于相似性而非效用检索，导致下游答案生成器需要去噪和重构证据，难以处理长对话历史中证据分散的问题。

Method: DeferMem采用段链接结构组织原始历史并在查询时检索广泛候选；使用DistillPO强化学习算法训练的记忆蒸馏器，将高召回但高噪声的候选蒸馏为忠实、自包含、查询条件的证据；DistillPO将证据蒸馏建模为包含消息选择和证据重写的结构化动作，通过分解门控奖励管道和结构对齐的优势分配进行优化。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上超越强基线，实现了最高QA准确率、最快运行时间，且记忆操作无需商用API令牌。

Conclusion: 将记忆检索与查询条件证据蒸馏解耦，以及使用强化学习优化后检索蒸馏，能显著提升LLM智能体在长期记忆问答任务上的表现和效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents still struggle with long-term memory question answering, where answer-supporting evidence is often scattered across long conversational histories and buried in substantial irrelevant content. Existing memory systems typically process memory before future queries are known, then retrieve the resulting units based on similarity rather than their utility for answering the query. This workflow leaves downstream answerers to denoise retrieved candidates and reconstruct query-specific evidence. We present DeferMem, a long-term memory framework that decouples this problem into high-recall candidate retrieval and query-conditioned evidence distillation. DeferMem uses a lightweight segment-link structure to organize raw history and retrieve broad candidates at query time. It then applies a memory distiller trained with DistillPO, our reinforcement learning algorithm for distilling the high-recall but highly noisy candidates into a set of faithful, self-contained, and query-conditioned evidence. DistillPO formulates post-retrieval evidence distillation as a structured action comprising message selection and evidence rewriting. It optimizes this action with a decomposed-and-gated reward pipeline and structure-aligned advantage assignment, gating reward components from validity to quality checks while exposing task-level correctness feedback early and assigning each reward to its responsible output span. On LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, DeferMem surpasses strong baselines in QA accuracy and memory-system efficiency, achieving the highest QA accuracy with the fastest runtime and zero commercial-API token cost for memory operations.

</details>


### [151] [Cohesion-6K: An Arabic Dataset for Analyzing Social Cohesion and Conflict in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22447)
*Aisha Ali Al-Athba,Wajdi Zaghouani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了Cohesion-6K数据集，包含6000条标注的阿拉伯语Facebook帖子，按从冲突到凝聚力的五类话语分类。研究发现冲突导向帖子获得的用户互动是解决方案导向帖子的2-4倍，揭示了阿拉伯社交媒体中分裂话语的显著可见性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注显性毒性检测，但社会凝聚力（即分裂与团结叙事之间的互动）的计算研究仍不足，尤其是在阿拉伯语社交媒体语境下。

Method: 构建Cohesion-6K数据集：从阿拉伯语Facebook收集与巴勒斯坦问题相关的帖子，结合人工专家判断和ChatGPT辅助预标注，由经过培训的标注者验证，最终获得6000条标注数据，标注为冲突、解决方案、社区参与、支持性互动、共享价值五类。

Result: 标注一致性达到Cohen's kappa=0.85；定量分析发现冲突导向帖子获得的用户互动是解决方案导向帖子的2-4倍（p<0.01），表明分裂话语在阿拉伯社交媒体中吸引不成比例的关注度。

Conclusion: Cohesion-6K为研究在线凝聚力和极化提供了透明可复用的资源，数据集、标注指南和预处理代码将以开放许可证发布，支持计算社会科学、数字传播和阿拉伯语自然语言处理领域的后续研究。

Abstract: The study of online discourse has become central to understanding societal polarization. While much research has focused on detecting overt toxicity, the subtle dynamics of social cohesion, meaning the interaction between divisive and unifying narratives, remain computationally underexplored (Bail, 2021; Gonzalez-Bailon and Lelkes, 2023). This paper presents Cohesion-6K, a manually and ChatGPT-assisted annotated dataset of six thousand Arabic public Facebook posts related to the Israeli Occupation of Palestine. Each post is assigned to one of five discourse categories that represent a continuum from conflict to cohesion: Conflict, Resolution, Community Engagement, Supportive Interactions, and Shared Values. The annotation process combines expert human judgment with model-assisted pre-labeling verified by trained annotators, achieving substantial inter-annotator agreement (Cohens kappa = 0.85). Quantitative analysis reveals a consistent engagement gap, where conflict-oriented posts receive between two and four times more user interaction than resolution-oriented ones (p < 0.01). This pattern illustrates how divisive discourse tends to attract disproportionate visibility in Arabic social media spaces. Cohesion-6K provides a transparent and reproducible resource for the study of online cohesion and polarization. The dataset, annotation guidelines, and preprocessing code will be released for research use under an open license, supporting future work in computational social science, digital communication, and Arabic natural language processing.

</details>


### [152] [From Correlation to Cause: A Five-Stage Methodology for Feature Analysis in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22462)
*Caleb Munigety*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出五阶段因果特征分析方法（探针设计→特征提取→因果验证→鲁棒性测试→部署集成），在GPT-2 Small的IOI任务上完整演示。发现稀疏自编码器恢复的特征仅部分因果（消融15个特征后98%提示仍准确），特征选择性比率与因果力反相关（r=-0.56），且因果特征在分布偏移下退化严重。部署评估显示最优监控配置可节省99.1%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多单独使用探针分析或因果干预，缺乏将特征发现、因果验证、鲁棒性测试和部署评估统一起来的端到端方法论，导致对特征因果作用的理解不完整。

Method: 五阶段方法论：(1)探针设计——激活修补恢复IOI电路；(2)特征提取——稀疏自编码器提取每名称选择性特征；(3)因果验证——消融实验+两种NLA启发评估（方差解释率、选择性-因果力相关性）；(4)鲁棒性测试——三种分布偏移下的电路传输与特征消融退化测试；(5)部署集成——基于成本模型的监控配置优化。

Result: (1)层9头9单独恢复+1.02；(2)特征效应量30-50激活单元；(3)消融15特征后98%提示准确；(4)选择性特征仅解释31%方差（SAE为99.7%），选择性比率与因果力r=-0.56；(5)电路干净迁移但特征消融效果退化；(6)最优配置$8.96/千次查询 vs $1000基线（节省99.1%）。

Conclusion: 单一阶段无法给出完整因果图景；特征的可检测性与因果性存在显著差距；五阶段联合分析为可解释AI提供了更可靠的评估框架，且部署成本优化对实际应用关键。

Abstract: We propose a five-stage methodology for causal feature analysis in transformer language models (probe design, feature extraction, causal validation, robustness testing, and deployment integration) and demonstrate it end-to-end on GPT-2 small performing the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. Activation patching recovers the canonical IOI circuit (layer-9 head 9 alone gives recovery +1.02). A sparse autoencoder recovers per-name selective features with effect sizes of 30 to 50 activation units. Causal validation finds these features specifically but only partially causal: ablating fifteen of them leaves the model accurate on 98% of prompts. Two NLA-inspired evaluations strengthen this picture: the fifteen selective features explain only 31% of activation variance versus the SAE's 99.7%, and selectivity ratio anticorrelates with causal force (r = -0.56). Robustness testing under three distribution shifts finds that the circuit transfers cleanly but feature ablation effects degrade substantially, exposing a gap between detection robustness and causal robustness. A cost-based deployment evaluation (assumed $50/FN, $0.42/FP, 2% error rate) finds an optimal monitor configuration yielding $8.96 per 1000 queries against a $1000 baseline, a 99.1% saving. Optimal composition strategy varies with cost ratio and base rate. The conjunction of stages produces findings no single stage would.

</details>


### [153] [Polite on the Surface, Wrong in Practice: A Curated Dataset for Fixing Honorific Failures in Multilingual Bangla Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22487)
*Md. Asaduzzaman Shuvo,Mahedi Hasan,Md. Tashin Parvez,Azizul Haque Noman,Md. Shafayet Hossain Ovi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多语言大模型在低资源语言（孟加拉语）中存在的语用鸿沟问题，构建了包含4,196个交互对的文化对齐指令微调数据集BLADE，并基于LoRA+NF4量化微调DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B等开源模型，实验表明模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在处理孟加拉语等低资源语言的結構变体、区域习语和敬语一致性时存在严重的语用鸿沟，缺乏文化对齐的数据集和评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含4,196个精心策划的交互对的文化对齐指令微调数据集BLADE，采用LoRA适配器+4-bit NF4量化框架对DeepSeek-8B和LLaMA-3.2-3B进行参数高效微调。

Result: 微调后的模型在结构保真度和敬语对齐方面取得实质性改进，为低资源多语言文本生成的语用差异弥合提供了严格基准。

Conclusion: 文化对齐的指令微调数据能有效弥合MLLM在低资源语言中的语用鸿沟，BLADE可作为评估和提升多语言模型文化感知能力的基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multilingual Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly enhanced cross-lingual conversational capabilities, yet modeling culturally nuanced and context-dependent communication remains a critical bottleneck. Specifically, existing state-of-the-art models exhibit a severe pragmatic gap when handling structural variations, regional idioms, and honorific consistencies in low-resource contexts like Bangla. To address this limitation, we introduce a novel, culturally aligned instruction-tuning dataset for \textbf{BangLa Application and DialoguE generation - BLADE} and benchmarking framework comprising $4,196$ meticulously curated interaction pairs. We leverage this resource to systematically fine-tune and evaluate leading open-weight architectures, including DeepSeek-8B and LLaMA-3.2-3B, utilizing parameter-efficient fine-tuning via LoRA adapters in a 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) quantization framework. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our dataset yield substantial improvements in structural fidelity and honorific alignment, providing a rigorous benchmark for bridging pragmatic disparities in low-resource multilingual text generation. Code and dataset: https://github.com/ashuvo25/Bangla_Application_LLM/tree/main

</details>


### [154] [BeLink: Biomedical Entity Linking Meets Generative Re-Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22501)
*Darya Shlyk,Stefano Montanelli,Lawrence Hunter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过指令微调开源生成式模型作为BEL流水线的重排序阶段，实现快速准确的候选选择，在多个基准上准确率提升3%-24%且推理时间更短，并集成到端到端系统BeLink中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的生物医学实体链接方法计算效率低、难以实际部署，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出集合式指令微调方法，对开源生成式模型进行指令微调，将其应用于BEL流水线的重排序阶段，实现高效准确的候选选择，并集成到模块化端到端系统BeLink中。

Result: 在多个BEL基准上，链接准确率提升3%-24%，同时推理时间相比现有最优方法有所降低。

Conclusion: 指令微调开源生成式模型作为重排序器是提升BEL效率与准确性的有效方案，BeLink系统适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, Biomedical Entity Linking (BEL) with large language models (LLMs) remains computationally inefficient and challenging to deploy in practical settings. In this work, we demonstrate that instruction-tuning of open-source generative models can offer an effective solution when applied at the re-ranking stage of the BEL pipeline. We propose a set-wise instruction-tuning formulation that enables fast and accurate candidate selection. Our method demonstrates strong performance on multiple BEL benchmarks, yielding significant improvements in linking accuracy (3%-24%) while reducing inference time compared to the state-of-the-art. We integrate our generative re-ranker into BeLink, a modular, end-to-end system designed for practical real-world BEL applications.

</details>


### [155] [Scene Abstraction for Lexical Semantics: Structured Representations of Situated Meaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22542)
*Yejin Cho,Katrin Erk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene Abstraction框架，通过构建词汇在上下文中的结构化场景表示（包含语境场景和表达轮廓），捕捉词汇隐含的情境化语义维度。基于该框架构建了COCA-Scenes数据集，实验表明场景识别准确率达82.4%，且场景轮廓比ATOMIC替代方案更符合人类对词汇情境化语义的理解（86.4%偏好）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇意义隐含丰富的情境化维度（如氛围、情感关联），但这些维度在现有计算语义表示中未被显式建模。咖啡与茶虽共享许多属性，却能唤起截然不同的场景与情感，这反映了词汇意义的系统性情境维度需要被结构化表征。

Method: 提出Scene Abstraction框架：每个场景由Contextual Scene（事件、实体、环境）和Expression Profile（参与事件、可概括属性、唤起情感）构成；通过大语言模型的少样本提示实现操作化；构建包含26个关键词、520个使用实例的COCA-Scenes数据集。

Result: 人类识别场景准确率82.4%，比基于文本嵌入的方法高11.8个百分点；场景轮廓在三个语义维度上以86.4%的偏好胜于ATOMIC替代方案，更符合人类对词汇上下文的理解。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化场景表征框架能有效捕捉词汇的情境化语义维度，场景具有跨人类观察者的可靠可识别性，且生成的场景轮廓优于现有常识知识库方法。

Abstract: Coffee and tea share many properties, yet they evoke strikingly different situations, atmospheres, and affective associations. These situated dimensions of word meaning are real and systematic, but they remain implicit in most computational representations of lexical meaning. We propose Scene Abstraction, a framework for constructing structured representations of the interpretive scenes that words participate in across usage contexts. Each scene consists of a Contextual Scene (Events, Entities, Setting) and an expression-centered Expression Profile (Engaged events, Generalizable properties, Evoked emotions), operationalized through few-shot prompting of a large language model. Our contributions are three-fold: (1) a structured representation framework for situated lexical meaning; (2) COCA-Scenes, a dataset of 520 usage instances across 26 keywords for distinct scene identification; and (3) empirical evidence from two experiments suggesting that scenes are reliably identifiable across human observers (82.4% accuracy, +11.8 pp over text-only embeddings) and that our scene profiles more closely align with human interpretation of words in context than ATOMIC-based alternatives (86.4% preference across three semantic dimensions).

</details>


### [156] [One prompt is not enough: Instruction Sensitivity Undermines Embedding Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22544)
*Yevhen Kostiuk,Kenneth Enevoldsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了指令嵌入模型对提示措辞的敏感性，发现单提示评估会严重误导模型性能报告和排行榜排名，任何模型都可以通过选择有利提示升至第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前指令嵌入模型使用单提示评估，忽略了模型对指令措辞的敏感性，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 在6个嵌入模型、11个数据集上，对每个数据集使用15个任务特定提示（共990个）进行系统性实验，分析分数分布和排名变化。

Result: 默认提示可能系统性低估或高估性能；排行榜排名对提示选择不鲁棒，任何模型通过选择有利提示均可升至第一。

Conclusion: 单提示评估不足以衡量指令调优嵌入模型，基准应包含多提示评估或同时报告敏感度与点估计。

Abstract: Instruction embedding models have become common among state-of-the-art models, however are evaluated using a single prompt per task. The single-point evaluation ignores a main problem of the instruction-based approach namely: sensitivity to the phrasing of the instruction. We present an empirical study of prompt sensitivity across 6 embedding models, 11 datasets, and 15 task-specific prompts per dataset, a total of 990. We show that reported scores misrepresent the distribution of scores over plausible prompts. The default prompt can both systematically understate or overstate performance. Furthermore, we show that the leaderboard ranking is not robust to prompt selection: by choosing prompts favorably, any model in our study can be promoted to first place. Our findings suggest that single-prompt evaluation is insufficient for instruction-tuned embedding models and that benchmarks should incorporate prompt robustness, either by evaluating over multiple prompts or by reporting sensitivity alongside point estimates.

</details>


### [157] [SynAE: A Framework for Measuring the Quality of Synthetic Data for Tool-Calling Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22564)
*Shuaiqi Wang,Aadyaa Maddi,Zinan Lin,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynAE是一个评估框架，用于衡量多轮工具调用智能体的合成测试基准在多大程度上复制和增强真实数据轨迹的特征，从有效性、保真度和多样性三个维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部生产数据集常因包含敏感信息或过于稀疏而无法用于测试，因此实践中越来越多用合成数据替代或增强真实数据。关键挑战在于量化合成数据集与真实数据之间的关系。

Method: SynAE从四个指标类别评估合成数据：(i)任务指令与中间响应、(ii)工具调用、(iii)最终输出、(iv)下游评估。通过真实可控的生成方案测试常见合成数据失效模式。

Result: SynAE能够检测数据有效性、保真度和多样性上的细粒度变化，并表明没有单一指标足以完整刻画合成数据质量，需要多轴评估。

Conclusion: 合成数据评估需要多维度、多指标的联合分析方法，SynAE为此提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: Today, tool-calling agents are commonly evaluated or tested on static datasets of execution traces, including input commands, agent responses, and associated tool calls. However, internal production datasets are often insufficient or unusable for testing; for example, they may contain sensitive or proprietary data, or they may be too sparse to support comprehensive testing (especially pre-deployment). In these settings, practitioners are increasingly replacing or augmenting real datasets with synthetic ones for evaluation purposes. A key challenge is quantifying the relation between these synthetic datasets and the real data. We introduce SynAE, an evaluation framework for assessing how well synthetic benchmarks for multi-turn, tool-calling agents replicate and augment the characteristics of real data trajectories. SynAE assesses the validity, fidelity, and diversity of synthetic data across four metric categories: (i) task instructions and intermediate responses, (ii) tool calls, (iii) final outputs, and (iv) downstream evaluation. We evaluate SynAE using recent agent benchmarks and test common synthetic data failure modes via realistic and controlled generation schemes. SynAE detects fine-grained variations in data validity, fidelity and diversity, and shows that no single metric is sufficient to fully characterize synthetic data quality, motivating a multi-axis evaluation of synthetic data for agent testing. A demo of SynAE is available at https://synae-2026-synae-demo.static.hf.space/index.html, with code at https://github.com/wsqwsq/SynAE.

</details>


### [158] [LANG: Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Reasoning with Language-Adaptive Hint Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22567)
*Yuchun Fan,Bei Li,Peiguang Li,Yilin Wang,Yongyu Mu,Jian Yang,Xin Chen,Rongxiang Weng,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Jingbo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LANG框架，通过语言条件提示引导非英语推理任务，结合渐进衰减计划和语言自适应切换机制，在提升多语言推理性能的同时保持语言一致性，且可推广至数学之外的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在多语言推理中存在输入语言一致性与推理质量之间的根本性权衡：保持输入语言一致性会损害推理质量，而优先推理又会导致语言向英语漂移。

Method: LANG框架包含两个关键机制：(1) 渐进衰减计划（progressive decay schedule），逐步撤除语言条件提示的支架作用；(2) 语言自适应切换（language-adaptive switch），根据特定语言难度定制学习视野。

Result: 在挑战性多语言数学基准上，LANG显著提升了推理性能且不损害语言一致性；该方法可推广至数学以外的任务，促进模型各层之间更一致的语言对齐。

Conclusion: LANG框架有效破解了多语言推理中语言一致性与推理质量之间的权衡困境，为多语言场景下的大语言模型推理增强提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for enhancing multi-step reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet its benefits have not fully translated to multilingual contexts. Existing methods struggle with a fundamental trade-off: prioritizing input-language consistency severely hampers reasoning quality, while prioritizing reasoning often leads to unintended language drift toward English. We address this challenge with LANG, a novel framework that leverages language-conditioned hints to guide exploration in non-English reasoning tasks. Our method incorporates two key mechanisms to prevent dependency on these hints: a progressive decay schedule that gradually withdraws scaffolding, and a language-adaptive switch that tailors learning horizons to specific language difficulties. Empirical results on challenging multilingual mathematical benchmarks reveal that LANG substantially enhances reasoning performance without compromising language consistency. Moreover, we show that our framework generalizes beyond mathematics, fostering more consistent language alignment across model layers

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Temperature: Hyperfitting as a Late-Stage Geometric Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22579)
*Meimingwei Li,Yuanhao Ding,Esteban Garces Arias,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"超拟合"（Hyperfitting）现象——在小型数据集上将LLM微调至接近零训练损失，出人意料地提升了开放生成质量并减少了贪婪解码中的重复。实验证明该现象本质上不同于温度缩放或静态词汇重加权，而是源于最终Transformer块中发生的"末端扩展"（Terminal Expansion），即特征空间的大幅几何扩张（约+80.8维），促进深度尾部token的提升。据此提出"晚期LoRA"策略，仅更新最后5层即可实现鲁棒生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现"超拟合"现象在提升生成质量上效果显著，但其底层机制尚不明确。由于超拟合导致极低熵的输出分布，让人怀疑其是否仅是简单的温度缩放等价物。本文旨在厘清超拟合的真正机制，区分其与分布锐化、静态词汇重加权等替代解释的差异。

Method: 1）熵匹配控制实验：对比超拟合与温度缩放对生成多样性的影响；2）消融研究：检验超拟合是否依赖静态词汇重加权；3）层分析：定位超拟合效应发生的具体Transformer层；4）提出Late-Stage LoRA策略：仅更新最后5层以验证末端效应的关键作用。

Result: 1）熵匹配实验表明温度缩放无法复现超拟合带来的多样性增益，证明二者本质不同；2）消融实验否定了静态词汇重加权假设，揭示超拟合依赖于动态、上下文相关的排序重排机制；3）层分析将效应定位到最终Transformer块中的"末端扩展"：特征空间维度扩张约+80.8，促进深度尾部token的激活；4）Late-Stage LoRA仅更新最后5层即取得鲁棒生成效果。

Conclusion: 超拟合并非简单的分布锐化或静态重加权，而是一种由最终Transformer层中特征空间几何扩张驱动的动态、上下文依赖的排序重排机制。基于此提出的Late-Stage LoRA策略以极小参数更新实现了高质量生成，为理解和利用超拟合现象提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a counterintuitive phenomenon termed "Hyperfitting", where fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) to near-zero training loss on small datasets surprisingly enhances open-ended generation quality and mitigates repetition in greedy decoding. While effective, the underlying mechanism remains poorly understood, with the extremely low-entropy output distributions suggesting a potential equivalence to simple temperature scaling. In this work, we demonstrate that this phenomenon is fundamentally distinct from distribution sharpening; entropy-matched control experiments reveal that temperature scaling fails to replicate the diversity gains of hyperfitting. Furthermore, we falsify the hypothesis of static vocabulary reweighting, showing through ablation studies that hyperfitting relies on a dynamic, context-dependent rank reordering mechanism. Layer-wise analysis localizes this effect to a "Terminal Expansion" in the final transformer block, where a substantial geometric expansion of the feature space (Delta Dim approx +80.8) facilitates the promotion of deep-tail tokens. Additionally, we introduce Late-Stage LoRA, a targeted fine-tuning strategy that updates only the final 5 layers, yielding robust generation with minimal parameter updates

</details>


### [160] [Agentic CLEAR: Automating Multi-Level Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22608)
*Asaf Yehudai,Lilach Eden,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Agentic CLEAR，一个自动、动态、易用的智能体评估框架，能在系统、轨迹、节点三个粒度生成文本洞察，与人工标注错误高度一致，并能预测任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估工具局限于基础可观测性和静态、手工制作的错误分类体系，无法适应新领域，缺乏对自主智能体行为的有效监督和评估手段。

Method: 设计Agentic CLEAR框架，运行在可观测性层之上，支持无缝集成；在系统级、轨迹级、节点级三个粒度上生成文本形式的评估洞察，并配备直观的UI界面。

Result: 在四个基准测试、七种智能体设置、数万次LLM调用实验中，Agentic CLEAR生成高质量、数据驱动的反馈，与人工标注错误高度一致，并能预测任务成功率。

Conclusion: Agentic CLEAR是一种自动、动态、易用的评估框架，有效解决了现有工具在可适应性和洞察深度方面的不足。

Abstract: Agentic systems are becoming more capable: agents define strategies, take actions, and interact with different environments. This autonomy poses serious challenges for overseeing and assessing agent behavior. Most current tools are limited, focusing on observability with basic evaluation capabilities or imposing static, hand-crafted error taxonomies that cannot adapt to new domains. To address this gap, we present Agentic CLEAR, an automatic, dynamic, and easy-to-use evaluation framework. It produces textual insights into the agent behavior on three levels of granularity: system, trace, and node. Agentic CLEAR operates above the observability layer, enabling seamless integration and featuring an intuitive UI that makes agent evaluation highly accessible. In our experiments on four benchmarks, seven agentic settings, and tens of thousands of LLM calls, we show that Agentic CLEAR produces high-quality, data-driven, insightful feedback. Our analysis shows strong alignment with human-annotated errors and the ability to predict task success rate.

</details>


### [161] [Chinese sensorimotor and embodiment norms for 3,000 lexicalized concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22616)
*Jing Chen,Gábor Parti,Yin Zhong,Chu-Ren Huang,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含3,000个汉语词汇的11维感觉运动规范和单维具身性规范的数据库（来自378名母语者），验证了感知具身强度（PSE）和Minkowski-3复合变量对词汇决策的预测能力，并通过回归分析发现感觉运动评分可从纯语言表征中部分恢复（平均Spearman r=0.62），表明分布语言使用编码了具身概念结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念知识如何植根于身体体验、机器系统能否在没有直接感觉运动经验的情况下获得此类知识，是认知科学和具身AI的核心问题。大规模规范资源对于实证研究至关重要，但非印欧语言的此类资源仍然稀缺。

Method: 1) 收集378名汉语母语者对3,000个词汇的11维感觉运动评分和单维具身性评分；2) 验证评分信度及与现有中文资源的交叉效度；3) 在词汇决策任务中测试感知具身强度（PSE）等复合变量的预测能力；4) 使用简单回归模型从语言表征恢复感觉运动评分；5) 采用表征相似性分析（RSA）比较感觉运动空间与语言表征空间的几何结构。

Result: 1) 评分呈现高信度和良好的交叉效度；2) PSE-Sensorimotor和Minkowski-3是词汇决策表现的最强预测因子；3) 感觉运动评分可从纯语言表征中部分恢复（平均Spearman r=0.62），但不同维度恢复程度差异显著（视觉和听觉维度高于化学感觉维度）；4) 感觉运动空间的几何结构可部分恢复（r=0.540）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，分布语言使用编码了具身概念结构的某些方面，支持了语言表征可部分捕捉概念具身性的观点；同时为中文词汇提供了大规模规范资源，对认知科学和具身AI研究具有重要价值。

Abstract: Understanding how conceptual knowledge is grounded in bodily experience, and to what extent machine systems can acquire such knowledge without direct sensorimotor experience, are central questions in both cognitive science and embodied artificial intelligence research. Large-scale normative resources are essential for investigating these questions empirically, yet such resources remain sparse for non-Indo-European languages. We present a novel normative database for 3,000 lexicalized concepts in Mandarin Chinese, comprising 11-dimensional sensorimotor ratings and unidimensional embodiment ratings collected from 378 native Mandarin speakers. The ratings demonstrate high reliability and strong cross-norm validity with existing Chinese resources, each of which covers fewer words and a subset of the 11 sensorimotor dimensions. In a validation study, we tested new variables derived from a theoretically motivated metric, Perceptual Strength of Embodiment (PSE) (Huang et al., 2025), together with seven common composite variables, on lexical decision tasks. The results suggest that PSE-Sensorimotor and Minkowski-3 are the strongest composite predictors of lexical decision performance, capturing the facilitatory effects of sensorimotor information on lexical processing. A further exploratory study showed that sensorimotor ratings are substantially recoverable from purely linguistic representations using simple regression models (mean Spearman r = .62 across dimensions), though recovery varied markedly: visual and auditory dimensions yielded higher correspondence than chemosensory ones. Representational similarity analysis further showed that the relational geometry of the sensorimotor space is also partially recoverable (r = .540), consistent with the view that distributional language use encodes aspects of embodied conceptual structure.

</details>


### [162] [More Context, Larger Models, or Moral Knowledge? A Systematic Study of Schwartz Value Detection in Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22641)
*Víctor Yeste,Paolo Rosso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在政治文本中检测Schwartz价值观时，上下文语境和外部道德知识的作用。结果表明：全文档上下文能提升监督模型（DeBERTa）3.8-4.8 macro-F1，但对零样本LLM帮助不一致；检索增强（RAG）在早期融合下对各模型均有稳定提升；模型规模扩大不保证性能增益。建议价值敏感NLP应联合评估上下文、知识和模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治文本中的Schwartz价值观检测具有挑战性，因为隐含线索往往依赖周围论证和相邻价值观之间的细微区分。作者旨在探究上下文语境和显式道德知识在句子级价值检测中何时能提供帮助。

Method: 采用ValuesML/Touché ValueEval格式，比较了句子级、窗口级和全文档三种输入设置；对比了无RAG和基于检索增强（使用精心整理的道义知识库）两种知识配置；使用了监督式DeBERTa-v3-base/large编码器以及12B到123B参数的零样本大语言模型。

Result: 全文档上下文使监督DeBERTa编码器提升3.8-4.8 macro-F1，但对零样本LLM无一致帮助；检索道德知识在早期融合下对每个测试模型族和上下文条件均有提升；模型规模扩大（DeBERTa-base到large、12B到更大LLM）不保证性能增益；简单早期融合优于测试的晚期融合和交叉注意力RAG变体。

Conclusion: 上下文和检索对社会情境性或概念易混淆的价值观帮助最大。价值敏感NLP应联合评估上下文、知识和模型族，而非将更长输入或更大模型视作通用改进手段。

Abstract: Detecting Schwartz values in political text is difficult because implicit cues often depend on surrounding arguments and fine-grained distinctions between neighboring values. We study when context and explicit moral knowledge help sentence-level value detection. Using the ValuesML/Touch{é} ValueEval format, we compare sentence, window, and full-document inputs; no-RAG and retrieval-augmented settings with a curated moral knowledge base; supervised DeBERTa-v3-base/large encoders; and zero-shot LLMs from 12B to 123B parameters. The results show that more context is not uniformly better: full-document context improves supervised DeBERTa encoders by 3.8--4.8 macro-F1 points over sentence-only input, but does not consistently help zero-shot LLMs. Retrieved moral knowledge is more consistently useful in matched comparisons, improving each tested model family and context condition under early fusion. However, scaling from DeBERTa-v3-base to large and from 12B to larger LLMs does not guarantee gains, and simple early fusion outperforms the tested late-fusion and cross-attention RAG variants for encoders. Per-value analyses show that context and retrieval help most for socially situated or conceptually confusable values. These findings suggest that value-sensitive NLP should evaluate context, knowledge, and model family jointly rather than treating longer inputs or larger models as universal improvements.

</details>


### [163] [Seeing the Poem: Image-Semantic Detection of AI-Generated Modern Chinese Poetry with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22654)
*Shanshan Wang,Fengying Ye,Hanjia Lyu,Caiwen Gou,Junchao Wu,Jingming Yao,Chengzhong Xu,Jiebo Luo,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图像-语义引导的现代中文诗歌检测方法，通过引入反映诗歌内容的图像，结合文本与视觉信息进行互补判断，显著提升了LLM作为诗歌检测器的性能，Gemini检测器达到了85.65%的Macro-F1分数，超越了传统检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM不能有效用作检测器，但这些研究未涉及现代中文诗歌；且没有相关研究探索LLM在现代中文诗歌检测中的表现。

Method: 提出图像-语义引导的诗歌检测方法，创新性地引入反映诗歌内容的图像，通过示例驱动方式有效整合图像中的意义、意象和情感等信息，与诗歌文本形成互补判断。

Result: 基于该方法的LLM检测器优于基于纯文本的基线检测器，甚至超过最佳传统检测器RoBERTa；Gemini检测器达到85.65%的Macro-F1分数，达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的图像-语义引导方法能有效提升LLM检测器性能，多个LLM检测器在不同LLM生成数据上的性能改进证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Previous detection studies have shown that LLMs cannot be effectively used as detectors, but these studies have not addressed modern Chinese poetry. Moreover, no relevant research has explored the performance of LLMs in detecting modern Chinese poetry. This paper evaluates and enhances the performance of LLMs as detectors for modern Chinese poetry, and proposes an image-semantic guided poetry detection method. Compared with traditional detection approaches, our method innovatively incorporates images that reflect the content of the poetry. Through example-driven approaches, our method effectively integrates information such as meaning, imagery, and feeling from the image, then forms a complementary judgment with the poem text. Experimental results demonstrate that the LLM detectors based on our method outperform baseline detectors based on plain text, and even surpass the best-performing traditional detector, RoBERTa. The Gemini detector using our method achieves a Macro-F1 score of 85.65%, reaching the state-of-the-art level. The performance improvements of different LLM detectors on multiple LLMs-generated data prove the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [164] [Moral Semantics Survive Machine Translation: Cross-Lingual Evidence from Moral Foundations Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22660)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以通过LLM翻译将英语的道德标注语料库迁移到波兰语等低资源语言。通过对约5万条社交媒体帖子进行系统验证（LaBSE相似度、CKA对齐、LLM评判、分类器一致性测试），发现尽管存在俚语和文化表达方面的不足，直接翻译仍能较好地保留道德语义特征（平均余弦相似度0.86，AUC差距仅0.01-0.02），微调后可进一步缩小差距。机器翻译是跨语言道德价值观研究的实用且经济有效的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道德语言具有细微性和文化变异性，跨语言翻译困难；而自动化道德价值观分类依赖几乎只存在于英语的语言特定标注语料库，使得其他语言在该领域严重资源不足。本文旨在探索是否能用LLM翻译填补这一空白。

Method: 以波兰语为测试案例，使用约5万条涵盖多样主题的道德标注社交媒体帖子，构建了包含四种方法的验证管线：(1) LaBSE跨语言嵌入相似度，(2) 中心核对齐(CKA)，(3) LLM作为评判者的评估，(4) 深度学习分类器一致性测试。

Result: 直接翻译后平均余弦相似度达0.86，AUC差距仅为0.01-0.02；微调语言模型后可进一步缩小差距。但翻译在处理俚语、粗俗语和文化负载表达方面仍存在不足。

Conclusion: 机器翻译是跨语言道德价值观研究的实用且经济有效的路径，研究结果有望泛化到其他相关语言。

Abstract: Moral language is subtle and culturally variable, making it difficult to translate faithfully across languages. Idiomatic expressions, slang, and cultural references introduce hard-to-avoid translation artifacts. Yet automated moral values classification depends on language-specific annotated corpora that exist almost exclusively in English. We investigate whether LLM-based translation can bridge this gap, taking Polish as a test case. Using $\sim$50k morally-annotated social media posts from a diverse range of topics, we apply a principled four-method validation pipeline: LaBSE cross-lingual embedding similarity, Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), LLM-as-judge evaluation, and deep learning classifier parity tests. We show that despite shortcomings in handling slang, vulgarity, and culturally-loaded expressions, direct translation preserves subtle moral cues well enough to be harvested by cross-lingual machine learning -- with mean cosine similarity of 0.86 and AUC gaps of 0.01--0.02 across all foundations closing further under fine-tuning of language models. These results demonstrate that machine translation is a practical and cost-effective path to moral values research in languages currently under-resourced in this domain. We demonstrate this for Polish as a representative Slavic language, with expected generalisation to related languages.

</details>


### [165] [Self-Policy Distillation via Capability-Selective Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22675)
*Guangya Hao,Yitong Shang,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao,Hanxue Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Policy Distillation (SPD)，一种无需外部信号即可实现通用、能力选择性的自蒸馏方法，通过提取低秩能力子空间并投影KV激活，在代码生成、数学推理和多项选择问答上提升高达16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自蒸馏方法要么依赖昂贵的外部信号（如正确性过滤、执行反馈），要么不加筛选地训练所有原始输出导致领域特定且难以泛化；同时，自生成输出将任务相关能力与风格模式、格式伪影、模型特定错误等纠缠在一起，削弱了目标能力的信号。

Method: SPD从模型自身在正确性定义令牌上的梯度中提取低秩能力子空间，在自生成过程中将KV激活投影到该子空间，然后对原始输出使用标准的下一个令牌预测损失进行微调。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和多项选择问答上，SPD相比最先进的自蒸馏方法提升高达13%，相比预训练基线提升高达16%；在域外泛化设置下提升15%。

Conclusion: SPD实现了无需外部信号、通用且能力选择性的自蒸馏，在多个任务上取得显著提升，并展现出优异的泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-distillation bootstraps large language models (LLMs) by training on their own generations. However, existing methods either rely on external signals to curate self-generated outputs (e.g., correctness filtering, execution feedback, and reward search), which are costly and unavailable for the best-performing frontier models, or skip curation entirely and train on all raw outputs, an approach that is often domain-specific and hard to generalize. Both also share a deeper weakness that self-generated outputs entangle task-relevant capability with others, such as stylistic patterns, formatting artifacts, and model-specific errors, diluting the signal for the specific capability one aims to improve. In this paper, we propose Self-Policy Distillation (SPD), which achieves generalizable, capability selective without any external signal. Specifically, SPD extracts a low-rank capability subspace from the model's own gradients on correctness-defining tokens, projects key-value (KV) activations into this subspace during self-generation, and fine-tunes on the resulting raw outputs with standard next-token prediction loss. Through extensive experiments across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice QA, we show that SPD achieves up to 13% improvement over state-of-the-art self-distillation methods without external signals and up to 16% improvement over pre-trained baselines. Notably, SPD demonstrates superior generalizability, achieving 15% better performance under out-of-domain generalization settings.

</details>


### [166] [Tokenization with Split Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22705)
*Craig W. Schmidt,Michael Krumdick,Adam Wiemerslage,Seth Ebner,Varshini Reddy,Yuval Pinter,Chris Tanner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToaST是一种基于分割树的新颖子词分词方法，通过整数规划优化词汇表选择，在英语文本上相比BPE等方法减少了11%以上的令牌数，并显著提升了语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的子词分词方法（如BPE、WordPiece、UnigramLM）在压缩效率和推理令牌数量上存在优化空间，作者希望直接优化压缩率以减少模型推理时的令牌数，从而扩展有效上下文长度。

Method: 提出Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST)方法：首先用预计算的字节n-gram计数将每个预分词贪心构建为满二叉树；推理时递归遍历分割树并发射路径上首个在词汇表中的节点；词汇表选择被形式化为整数规划(IP)问题，最小化所有分割树上的总令牌数，其线性规划(LP)松弛在实践中接近整数解，训练时间复杂度与分割树数量呈二次关系。

Result: 在词汇量≥40,960时，ToaST相比BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少了超过11%的令牌数；单字节令牌使用频率更低，Renyi效率显著提升；在1.5B参数语言模型实验中，ToaST取得了最高的CORE分数，比基线提升2.6%-7.6%，其中两个基线对比显著，并在22个任务中13个上取得最佳。

Conclusion: ToaST通过直接优化压缩率的整数规划方法，在令牌效率和下游任务性能上均优于主流分词方法，有效延长了模型的上下文长度。

Abstract: We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.

</details>


### [167] [ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22734)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Farzaneh Firoozbakht,Lukas Galke Poech,Jan Baumbach,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个具有时间线索的三元组的时序生物医学知识图谱，覆盖13,431种疾病。通过多智能体流水线从文献中提取知识，并引入ChronoTQA基准测试。实验表明，时序问题对前沿大语言模型造成约30%的性能下降，而ChronoMedKG的检索增强可挽回47-65%的长尾失败，远优于传统KG（17-29%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学知识图谱（如PrimeKG、Hetionet、iKraph）将疾病关联视为静态事实，缺乏时间信息（如症状在不同年龄段对应不同疾病），限制了其在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的应用。

Method: 通过疾病自主的多智能体流水线构建：多个前沿大语言模型独立从PubMed/PMC文献中提取知识，仅保留多模型共识、经可信度过滤和本体对齐的三元组。每个关联绑定时间组件（发病窗口、进展阶段），附有PMID可追溯证据和多信号可信度评分。

Result: ChronoMedKG包含460,497个三元组（从1,300万原始提取中过滤），覆盖13,431种疾病，与Orphadata一致性达92.7%。为6,250种疾病添加了时间信息，包括1,657种罕见病。ChronoTQA基准包含3,341个问答。前沿LLM在时序问题上性能下降约30%，ChronoMedKG检索增强挽回47-65%的长尾失败，HPOA-RAG仅挽回17-29%。

Conclusion: ChronoMedKG为检索增强临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴，显著提升了模型在时序临床推理任务上的表现。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation.
  We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment.
  ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.

</details>


### [168] [Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22769)
*Pilchen Hippolyte,Fabre Romain,Signe Talla Franck,Perez Patrick,Grave Edouard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练数据顺序对大语言模型时间敏感知识获取的影响，通过构建7000多个时间锚定问题的基准，并预训练6B参数模型，发现按时间顺序训练能提升知识的新鲜度和时间精准度，同时保持通用语言理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常在打乱语料上训练，导致知识冻结在训练时，时间锚定能力不明确。现有工作缺乏对数据顺序如何影响时间敏感知识获取的系统研究。

Method: 构建超过7000个时间锚定问题的基准和评估协议；在时间排序的Common Crawl快照上预训练6B参数模型，与标准打乱预训练进行对比分析。

Result: 顺序训练模型在通用语言理解和常识方面与打乱基线相当，但知识更新、时间精准度更优；顺序训练提升事实新鲜度，而打乱训练在老数据上表现更好（可能因重复更多）。

Conclusion: 时间有序预训练能有效提升模型的时间敏感知识获取能力，为LLM持续学习研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

</details>


### [169] [Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22771)
*Long Phan,Devin Kim,Alexander Pan,Alice Blair,Adam Khoja,Dan Hendrycks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs存在隐蔽政治偏见（反向话题不对称处理），提出情感一致性和帮助一致性两类指标，并通过政治一致性训练（PCT）的强化学习方法有效减少偏见，同时保持模型整体有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在敏感话题上的显性政治偏见，但忽视了模型在处理对立政治立场的反向话题时存在的不对称性（隐蔽政治偏见）。作者旨在系统量化并减少这类更隐蔽的偏见形式。

Method: 1. 识别出7类隐蔽政治偏见技术。2. 提出情感一致性（衡量措辞和框架的对称性）和帮助一致性（衡量回答深度和参与度的对称性）两个指标。3. 提出政治一致性训练（PCT），包含情感一致性训练和帮助一致性训练两种互补范式的强化学习方法。

Result: PCT在保持模型整体有用性的同时，显著降低了隐蔽政治偏见，并且能够泛化到留出基准测试中。

Conclusion: 隐蔽政治偏见是LLM中一个值得关注的问题，PCT作为一种有效的去偏方法能够在不影响模型性能的前提下实现泛化性偏见缓解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai

</details>


### [170] [Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22785)
*Mirac Suzgun,Emily Shen,Federico Bianchi,Alexander Spangher,Thomas Icard,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Jurafsky,James Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对6款AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5等）在6种语言区域、2100个事实性问题上的14天评估显示：最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，但自由回答下降11-13%；存在三个核心失败模式：印地语准确率最低（79%）且检索存在英语偏好，70%以上错误源于检索失败而非推理错误，在包含虚假前提的问题上准确率从88-96%骤降至19-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人正迅速改变人们获取新闻的方式，但尚无研究系统评估这些带有专有搜索集成和检索-合成管道的系统如何跨语言和地区处理新兴事实。

Method: 在14天（2026年2月9-22日）内，基于BBC同日新闻报道，构建2100个事实性问题，覆盖6个区域服务（美加、阿拉伯、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语），对Gemini 3 Flash/Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5和GPT-4o mini六款AI聊天机器人进行多项选择和自由回答两种格式的评估。

Result: 最佳系统多项选择准确率超90%，自由回答下降11-13%；所有模型印地语准确率最低（79% vs 89-91%）；70%以上错误由检索失败驱动；含虚假前提的问题准确率从88-96%降至19-70%，最脆弱模型64%接受虚假事实。

Conclusion: 高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不公、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖，以及面对用户提出的不完美查询时的脆弱性。前提检测与答案恢复是部分独立的能力，存在检测-准确率悖论。

Abstract: AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.

</details>


### [171] [Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22821)
*Jan Tempus,Philip Whittington,Craig W. Schmidt,Dennis Komm,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种将分词器构建形式化为线性规划并使用凸优化求解的新算法ConvexTok，相比传统贪婪算法（BPE/Unigram），能持续改进内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte，并可在常见词汇量下达到最优解的1%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分词算法（如BPE和Unigram）是贪婪算法，只做局部最优决策，不考虑整个词汇表的全局效果，缺乏最优性保证。

Method: 将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，使用凸优化工具求解，得到新算法ConvexTok。

Result: ConvexTok持续改进了内在分词指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte；在下游任务上也有改进但不够一致；可以在常见词汇量下达到最优解的1%以内。

Conclusion: 凸优化方法可以替代贪婪算法进行分词器构建，提供更优的分词质量和可认证的最优性保证。

Abstract: Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Flying Together: Human-Guided Immersive Shared Control for Aerial Robot Teams in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21680)
*Lou De Bel-Air,Luca Morando,Ruitao Chen,Keru Wang,Benjamin Jarvis,Charbel Toumieh,Yang Zhou,Ken Perlin,Dario Floreano,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于虚拟现实的共享控制框架，用于无人机编队在受限未知环境中的实时用户引导探索，通过用户引导的运动基元规划器和导纳控制器实现人机协同导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多机器人系统在适应未知条件和捕捉操作者目标方面存在不足，尤其是在非结构化环境中难以灵活调整行为以满足人为意图。

Method: 提出了一种基于VR的共享控制框架，核心包括：(1) 用户引导的运动基元规划器，计算连续无碰撞轨迹并集成操作者输入；(2) 导纳控制器，使操作者灵活影响编队行为；(3) 双边VR界面，通过迁移点引导并接收即时视觉反馈；(4) 支持物理与仿真混合现实操作。

Result: 实验结果表明，共享控制提升了障碍物规避能力、维持了机间间距，并降低了操作者负担。

Conclusion: 证明了沉浸式人机协同多机器人导航的可行性和优势，为未知环境下的无人机编队操作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While autonomous multi-robots can achieve safe and coordinated navigation, they often struggle to adapt to unforeseen conditions and to capture operator-driven objectives in unstructured environments. We present a Virtual Reality (VR)-based shared control framework for teams of drones operating in constrained and unknown environments, enabling real-time, user-guided exploration. At the core of our approach is a novel, user-guided motion-primitive-based planner that computes continuous, collision-free trajectories while continuously integrating operator input. This planner is coupled with an admittance controller, allowing the operator to flexibly influence team behavior and guide drones toward regions of interest that autonomous planners may overlook. The system supports mixed-reality operations with both physical and simulated drones, and implements a bilateral VR-based interface, allowing the operator to guide the robot team via migration points while receiving immediate visual feedback of the team state. Experimental results show that shared control improves obstacle avoidance, maintains inter-agent spacing, and reduces operator effort, demonstrating the feasibility and advantages of immersive, human-in-the-loop multi-robot navigation.

</details>


### [173] [Distributed Multi-Coverage for Robot Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21686)
*Mariem Guitouni,Aaron T. Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自主无人机蜂群在监视、环境监测和基础设施检查等任务中需保持可靠覆盖的问题，提出了一种分布式多覆盖算法，使机器人仅通过本地感知、本地通信即可运行，无需全局协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中机器人面临通信范围有限、机载计算能力受限、局部故障不能导致任务终止等约束，而现有最优集中式方法无法满足这些分布式需求。

Method: 提出一种分布式多覆盖算法，机器人仅依靠局部感知和局部通信运行，无全局协调机制。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: 所提分布式算法能够在机器人蜂群中实现关键资产的多覆盖冗余保障，克服了实际部署中的通信、计算和容错约束。

Abstract: Autonomous drone swarms deployed for surveillance, environmental monitoring, and infrastructure inspection must maintain reliable coverage of critical assets despite robot failures. This requires multicoverage: each asset must be observed by multiple robots for redundancy, with coverage requirements varying by asset importance. While recent work has solved the centralized problem optimally using integer programming, practical deployments face constraints that demand distributed solutions: robots operate with limited communication ranges, onboard computation restricts global planning, and partial system failures must not cause mission abort. We present a distributed multicoverage algorithm for robot swarms operating with local sensing, local communication, and no global coordination.

</details>


### [174] [Closed-Loop Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Deformable Microfiber Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21688)
*Alessandro Amici,Houari Bettahar,Veeti Jaakkola,Quan Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭环仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的强化学习方法，用于微纤维在表面上的形状控制。核心思想是在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练几何形状调节策略，部署时依赖实时视觉反馈迭代修正未建模的表面相互作用。该策略无需再训练或域自适应，直接迁移到40Hz的物理双夹持微操作系统中，在多种纤维直径和操作长度下均实现了亚毫米级形状误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主接触式微操作因微尺度表面和界面相互作用难以精确建模，传统的基于模型的控制和仿真到现实的学习方法受到限制，亟需一种能克服建模不准确性的有效控制方法。

Method: 采用闭环sim-to-real强化学习方法：在简化无摩擦仿真器中训练几何形状调节策略；部署时利用实时视觉反馈形成闭环，迭代修正因未建模表面相互作用产生的实际偏差；训练后直接零样本迁移到物理双夹持微操作系统。

Result: 在24种不同初始配置下，平均点状形状误差为270±80μm；覆盖3种纤维直径（50,80,120μm）和3种操作长度（10,15,20mm）共9种样本组合，均实现亚毫米级最终形状误差，无需重新训练或调参。

Conclusion: 在简化仿真器中学习的策略，只要仿真到现实差距中与任务相关的效应在闭环反馈中保持可观测和可修正，就能在表面接触下实现可重复的真实世界微纤维形状调节。

Abstract: Autonomous contact-based micromanipulation is challenging because surface and interfacial interactions at the microscale are difficult to model accurately, limiting the use of conventional model-based control and sim-to-real learning. We present a closed-loop sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) approach for microfiber shape control on a surface. The central idea is to train geometric shape regulation in a simplified frictionless simulator and rely on real-time visual feedback during deployment to iteratively correct the observed effects of unmodeled surface interactions. An RL policy trained entirely in simulation is transferred directly to a physical dual-gripper micromanipulation system operating at 40 Hz, without retraining or domain adaptation. Using silk microfibers as a testbed, the policy achieves a mean point-wise shape error of 270 $\pm$ 80 $μ$m across twenty-four diverse initial configurations. Across nine specimens covering all combinations of three fiber diameters (50, 80, and 120 $μ$m) and three manipulated lengths (10 mm, 15mm, and 20 mm), the same policy achieves sub-millimeter final shape error without any retraining or retuning. These results show that a policy learned in a simplified simulator can achieve repeatable real-world microfiber shape regulation under surface contact, provided that the task-relevant effects of the sim-to-real mismatch remain observable and correctable within the closed feedback loop.

</details>


### [175] [Motion Design for Grasp-Based Dynamic Locomotion in Microgravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21704)
*Chaerim Moon,Joohyung Kim,Justin K. Yim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对微重力环境下多肢体机器人抓取式动态运动，提出了设计参数（步态模式、步幅、速度、标称姿态）和参数化规划框架，通过两种四足形态的仿真验证，发现扩大可行接触力空间和抑制冲击性全身动力学可提升运动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微重力环境下的锚点稀疏且不规则，传统运动方式受限；需要多肢体抓取式运动才能实现动态运动，但需同时调节接触交互和全身协调，现有研究缺乏系统性的设计参数分析。

Method: 提出参数化运动规划框架，支持步态模式、步幅、速度、标称姿态等参数变化；采用两种代表性四足形态进行物理仿真评估，从稳定性和驱动需求两个维度衡量运动性能。

Result: 扩大可行接触力空间和抑制冲击性全身动力学能够改善运动性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现为微重力环境下多肢体系统的接触配置选择和全身协调策略提供了指导。

Abstract: Locomotion in microgravity often relies on sparsely and irregularly arranged anchors, motivating grasp-based mobility with multiple limbs. In this setting, dynamic locomotion is feasible only through deliberate regulation of both anchored interactions and whole-body coordination under coupled dynamic and kinematic constraints. This paper presents design insights for grasp-based dynamic locomotion with multi-limbed robotic systems in microgravity, targeting scenarios that require 6D limb manipulation to establish contacts with candidate anchors. The investigated design parameters include gait pattern, stride length, locomotion speed, and nominal posture. A parameterizable locomotion planning framework is proposed to support variations of these parameters and to evaluate the resulting locomotion performance in terms of stability and actuation demand. Two representative quadruped morphologies are adopted for evaluation in physics-based simulation. The results demonstrate that enlarging the feasible contact wrench space and attenuating impulsive whole-body dynamics improve locomotion performance. These findings inform strategies for contact configuration selection and whole-body coordination in microgravity locomotion with multi-limbed systems.

</details>


### [176] [PGDG: Physically Grounded Data Generation for Robust Bimanual Policy Learning from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21710)
*Cunxi Dai,Haoran Chang,Aditya Nisal,Rahul Kumar,Guofei Chen,Tao Chen,Yuzhe Qin,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PGDG提出了一种无需人工标注的数据生成框架，通过物理仿真采样器与数据集策展器迭代协作，将单条人类演示扩展为涵盖多样化恢复行为的紧凑数据集，显著提升双臂灵巧操作任务中的模仿学习鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的双臂灵巧操作的模仿学习面临两大挑战：多样化的演示数据采集成本高昂，且微小扰动会使系统陷入离线状态（off-manifold states），而缺乏恢复行为的监督信号。

Method: PGDG框架包含两个核心组件：（1）物理驱动的采样器（physics-grounded sampler），从更新后的分布中采样物理可行的轨迹候选；（2）数据集策展器（dataset curator），选择信息量大、非冗余且可恢复的行为，更新采样分布以覆盖未充分探索的恢复模式。此外，采用短视距采样控制对选中的高风险状态重新标注纠正动作。

Result: 在四项双臂操作任务中，PGDG在仿真和零样本真实世界迁移中均优于空间增强方法。以RotateBox-Pitch为例，仿真成功率从38%提升至93%，真实世界从35%提升至82%。同时，PGDG还可用于微调GR00T等基础模型，成功率从46%提升至77%。

Conclusion: PGDG通过零样本策展从单条演示生成高质量恢复行为数据集，有效解决了双臂操作中数据稀缺与离线状态问题，并具备与基础模型结合的扩展能力。

Abstract: Behavior cloning for contact-rich bimanual manipulation remains challenging because diverse demonstrations are expensive to collect, and even small disturbances can push the system into off-manifold states where no recovery supervision is available. We propose PGDG, a data generation framework with zero-shot curation that expands a single demonstration into a compact dataset of physically plausible, successful, and diverse recovery behaviors without additional human labeling. PGDG iterates between a physics-grounded sampler and a dataset curator, where the curator selects informative, non-redundant, and recoverable behaviors to update the sampling distribution toward under-covered recovery modes, and the sampler draws physically plausible rollout candidates from this updated distribution and retains successful trajectories. To further improve data quality, PGDG applies short-horizon sampling-based control to relabel selected risky states with corrective actions. Across four bimanual manipulation tasks, PGDG consistently outperforms spatial-only augmentation in both simulation and zero-shot real-world transfer. On RotateBox-Pitch, success improves from 38% to 93% in simulation and from 35% to 82% in the real world. PGDG also enables effective foundation models fine-tuning such as GR00T, increasing success from 46% to 77%. Additional results are available in our website: https://cunxid.github.io/PGDG/.

</details>


### [177] [Mind the Gaps: Multi-Robot Feedback-Driven Ergodic Coverage in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21719)
*Thales Costa Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应覆盖策略，利用实时环境模型反馈构建目标信息分布，增强传统遍历轨迹优化，使机器人团队在未知先验分布环境下动态优先覆盖高感兴趣区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遍历搜索方法在给定目标分布时能有效探索，但未能考虑环境的未知先验分布，导致在动态采样任务中机器人分配效率低下。

Method: 基于环境参数化模型的实时反馈构建目标空间信息分布，在线更新模型，增强传统遍历轨迹优化方法，使机器人动态调整采样行为，优先覆盖高兴趣区域。

Result: 通过仿真验证了该方法在提升覆盖效率和资源分配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能在未知先验分布环境下提高多机器人覆盖效率，优化资源利用，适用于静态或缓慢变化的环境。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of multi-robot adaptive coverage, where teams of robots perform dynamic sampling by continuously adjusting their positions to collect data in an environment. This task can be challenging, particularly when robots must be efficiently allocated to new sampling locations over time. Ergodic search methods optimize robot trajectories by ensuring that the robots' time-averaged spatial distribution aligns with the spatial distribution of environmental information. While these methods promote effective exploration provided a target distribution, they often fail to account for unknown prior distributions of the environment. To overcome this limitation, we propose an adaptive coverage strategy that utilizes real-time feedback from an environmental model to adjust robot sampling behavior in response to unknown conditions. Our approach enhances traditional ergodic trajectory optimization by constructing a target spatial information distribution based on parametric models of the environment, which are updated online. This strategy assumes that the environment is either static or changes slowly compared to the robot's motion. Our framework allows robots to dynamically prioritize regions of high interest, improving coverage efficiency, synthesizing effective control policies for individual agents, and optimizing resource use in settings with unknown prior distributions. We validate our approach through simulations, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing coverage and resource allocation.

</details>


### [178] [Safe and Steerable Geometric Motion Policies for Robotic Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21811)
*Albert Wu,Riccardo Bonalli,Thomas Lew,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafePBDS是一个几何一致性框架，通过拉回控制屏障函数将任务流形上的安全约束转化为配置流形加速度的线性约束，并引入任务流形动作接口实现高层策略的低维干预，在灵巧操作中取得92.5%抓取成功率和360°以上掌面内重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧操作需要协调定义在异构几何空间（如配置流形R^7、末端执行器位姿SE(3)、避障边界R）中的目标和约束，现有方法缺乏几何一致性框架来统一处理这些不同空间的安全约束。

Method: 1) 提出拉回控制屏障函数（Pullback CBF），将任务流形上的安全条件反向映射为配置流形加速度的线性约束；2) 设计任务流形动作接口，允许高层策略注入低维残差运动，零输入时恢复自主行为，任意输入下仍保持安全性。

Result: 在Franka Panda-Allegro Hand平台验证：20个日常物品120次试验抓取成功率92.5%；通过一维动作接口排除任意一个手指进行3指抓取，36次试验成功率94.4%；实现首个模型驱动的全驱动掌面内手内重定向，双向偏航旋转超360°。

Conclusion: SafePBDS在几何一致性框架下实现了可证明安全的灵巧操作，高效规划能力支持手内重定向，动作接口兼顾了高层策略的灵活性与底层自主行为的安全性。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous manipulation requires continuously reconciling objectives and constraints defined on heterogeneous geometric spaces: a robot controlled on a $\mathbb{R}^7$ configuration manifold may need to track end effector poses on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ while satisfying obstacle avoidance margins in $\mathbb{R}$. We present Safe Pullback Bundle Dynamical Systems (SafePBDS), a geometrically consistent framework that computes optimal, certifiably safe configuration manifold accelerations from objectives and safety requirements on arbitrary task manifolds. SafePBDS builds on prior work that combines predefined task manifold dynamical systems to produce autonomous motion. Its first innovation is a pullback control barrier function construction, which converts task manifold safety conditions into linear constraints on configuration manifold accelerations. The second innovation is a task manifold action interface that allows a high-level policy to inject low dimensional residual motions; zero input recovers the autonomous behavior, while safety is preserved under arbitrary inputs. This lets high-level policies efficiently steer exploration while leaving precise motion to the autonomous behavior. We validate SafePBDS in simulation and on a 23-DOF Franka Panda-Allegro Hand platform. On dexterous grasping, SafePBDS achieves a $92.5\%$ success rate across 20 household objects and 120 trials. Using the action interface, the method can exclude any one of the four fingers during grasping via a one-dimensional action, achieving $94.4\%$ 3-finger grasp success across 3 objects and 36 trials. The efficient planning and safety guarantee of SafePBDS also enables the first model-based, fully actuated palm-down in-hand reorientation, exceeding $360^\circ$ of yaw rotation in both directions under varying object weight and wrist motion. Demo video and details: https://tml.stanford.edu/safe-pbds

</details>


### [179] [Analytical and Experimental Force Analysis of a Soft Linear Pneumatic Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21836)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对线性软 sleeve 致动器（LSSA）提出了一种准静态解析模型，通过实验验证了其在压力、几何、位移、载荷和轴向刚度耦合作用下的力生成行为，揭示了输出力随伸长增加而下降、外部载荷延迟力生成等关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软 sleeve 致动器（SSA）虽已应用于可穿戴机器人，但其力生成行为（尤其是输出力随伸长的变化、外部载荷的影响及轴向刚度的机械作用）尚未得到充分解释，因此需要建立理论模型并进行实验分析。

Method: 建立了准静态解析模型，将净轴向力表达为帽部和折叠壁产生的压力贡献减去轴向刚度相关的力；通过预设伸长和静态载荷实验评估致动器响应。

Result: 125 kPa 下，力从零伸长时的约112 N 降至40 mm时的接近零；静态载荷延迟了可测力生成并降低了力输出，尤其在低中压时明显。

Conclusion: LSSA 的力生成受压力、几何、位移、载荷和轴向刚度的耦合效应控制。

Abstract: Soft sleeve actuators (SSAs) have recently been developed as a pneumatic actuation approach for wearable and assistive robotic systems. By integrating the actuation structure into a sleeve-like geometry, these actuators can reduce reliance on external attachment layers and transmission mechanisms while maintaining compliance with limb-shaped surfaces. However, the force-generation behavior of SSAs remains insufficiently explained, particularly with respect to the variation of output force during extension, the influence of external loading, and the mechanical role of axial stiffness. This paper presents an analytical and experimental force analysis of a linear soft sleeve actuator (LSSA). A quasi-static analytical model was developed by expressing the net axial force as the pressure-generated contribution from the cap and folded walls, reduced by the force associated with axial stiffness. The model incorporates internal pressure, projected pressure areas, folded wall geometry, axial displacement, and an experimentally fitted axial stiffness relation. Prescribed-extension and static-load experiments were conducted to evaluate the actuator response. At 125 kPa, the generated force decreased from approximately 112 N at zero extension to nearly zero at 40 mm. Static loading delayed measurable force generation and reduced force output, particularly at low and intermediate pressures. The results show that LSSA force generation is governed by coupled effects of pressure, geometry, displacement, loading, and axial stiffness.

</details>


### [180] [EvoScene-VLA: Evolving Scene Beliefs Inside the Action Decoder for Chunked Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21862)
*Chushan Zhang,Ruihan Lu,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoScene-VLA，通过在VLA策略中引入持久化的、由动作更新的场景先验，使得每次控制调用时，场景状态既包含当前视觉观测，也包含上一动作块产生的场景更新。训练时使用场景预测器和几何锚点进行辅助学习，部署时丢弃。在31个RoboTwin任务和真实机器人上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分块VLA策略每次更新仅依赖当前视觉观测，但机器人动作会导致接触、遮挡和物体运动，使得关键几何信息在下一视觉更新到来前发生变化。空间VLA改进当前帧几何，时序VLA聚合过去帧，但都无法跨动作块维持一个由动作更新的场景先验。

Method: 提出EvoScene-VLA，其核心是循环场景前缀（recurrent scene prefix），在VLM调用时，将当前观测场景信息与上一动作块的由动作更新得到的场景先验结合；动作解码器同时输出下一动作块和紧凑的场景更新，该更新成为下一轮的先验。训练时引入场景预测器（Scene Predictor）提供未来场景token目标，以及几何锚点（Geometric Anchor）将场景槽与冻结的深度和3D教师对齐，部署时两者均丢弃。

Result: 在31个RoboTwin任务上，固定评估成功率从87.2%提升至89.1%，随机化评估从86.1%提升至88.5%。在Galaxea R1-Lite真实机器人上，EvoScene-VLA优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 跨控制调用维持动作更新的持久化场景先验能有效提升VLA策略的性能，尤其在涉及接触和物体运动的场景中。辅助训练模块可在部署时移除，不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Chunked vision-language-action (VLA) policies predict multi-step robot controls, conditioning each update on the current visual observation alone. Yet robot actions cause contact, occlusion, and object motion, and the geometry that later decisions depend on can change before the next visual update arrives. Spatial VLAs improve current-frame geometry. Temporal VLAs aggregate past frames. Neither maintains an action-updated scene prior across chunks. We argue for a persistent action-updated scene state across control calls, and introduce EvoScene-VLA. Its recurrent scene prefix carries a geometry-aware scene state across chunks. At each vision-language model (VLM) call, the VLM combines scene information from the current observation with the action-updated prior from the previous chunk; the action decoder outputs both the next action chunk and a compact scene update. This update becomes the next prior, which the VLM corrects against the new observation when the next call arrives. Each control call therefore starts from a scene prior that reflects both recent actions and fresh visual evidence. During training, \textbf{Scene Predictor} supplies future scene-token targets, and Geometric Anchor aligns scene slots with frozen depth and 3D teachers. We discard both modules at deployment. On 31 RoboTwin tasks, EvoScene-VLA raises average success from 87.2% to 89.1% in fixed evaluation and from 86.1% to 88.5% in randomized evaluation. On the Galaxea R1-Lite real robot, EvoScene-VLA outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Higher Order Reasoning for Collaborative Communicationless Mobile Robot Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21901)
*Jonathan Reasoner,Nicola Bezzo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态认知规划框架，使机器人在无通信环境下能够通过高阶推理进行隐式协调和长期规划，并利用行为树和时序感知MPPI控制器实现低层执行。仿真和实物实验表明，该方法相比一阶基线显著缩短了任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无通信环境中，多机器人系统无法依赖常规的信息交换进行协调，现有策略在通信受限场景下鲁棒性不足，亟需一种不依赖显式通信的隐式协调机制。

Method: 1）构建并传播高阶认知信念粒子；2）使用贝叶斯推理更新世界信念；3）通过行为树选择动作，同时预测队友决策；4）利用时序感知的MPPI控制器将推理集成到低层轨迹执行中，实现部分可观测下的拦截和轨迹自适应。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，所提框架相比一阶基线方法一致地减少了任务完成时间，验证了认知逻辑在通信受限多机器人协调中的有效性。

Conclusion: 认知逻辑可以作为通信限制环境下多机器人系统鲁棒协调的坚实基础，动态认知规划框架能够有效实现隐式协调并提升任务效率。

Abstract: In communicationless environments, multi-robot systems must operate without the constant information exchange that many coordination strategies typically assume. This paper presents a novel dynamic epistemic planning framework that enables implicit coordination and long horizon planning through higher-order reasoning among robots. With our approach, robots form and propagate higher-order belief particles, update world beliefs using Bayesian inference, and select actions via a behavior tree that anticipates teammates' likely decisions. A temporally aware Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller integrates this reasoning into low-level execution, allowing robots to plan intercepts and adapt trajectories under partial observability. The proposed framework is evaluated in both simulations and physical experiments, where it consistently reduces task completion time compared to a first-order baseline, demonstrating that epistemic logic can serve as a robust foundation for resilient coordination in communication-restricted domains.

</details>


### [182] [Non-Contact Vibration-Based Damage Detection of Civil Structures Using a Cost-Effective Autonomous UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21914)
*Javier Becerril,Maximiliano Vargas,Jennifer Herrera,Joanna Gutierrez,Jorge Rios,Mohsen Amjadian,Constantine Tarawneh,Jinghao Yang,Qi Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低成本自主无人机（UAV）的非接触式振动结构损伤检测方法，通过视觉运动跟踪从视频中提取振动信号识别固有频率变化。实验验证了多种平台（智能手机、USB相机、定制无人机）均能成功捕获频率及其损伤偏移，无人机误差略高（最高5.7%），但能可靠检测损伤，且成本远低于商业系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统接触式振动检测在复杂或危险环境中难以实施，现有商用无人机系统成本高昂。本文旨在开发一种低成本、自主化的无人机平台，实现非接触式结构健康监测，特别是在GPS受限环境下仍能工作，并验证其检测可行性。

Method: 采用视觉运动跟踪从视频中提取振动位移时程，通过频域分析识别固有频率变化。实验使用两台智能手机、一台USB相机和一台搭载自主对准系统的低成本无人机，在实验室框架结构上进行健康与模拟损伤两种状态的测试，结果与接触式加速度计及有限元模型进行对比验证。

Result: 所有平台均能成功捕获结构基频及其因损伤引起的频率偏移。无人机平台由于平台诱导扰动和传感限制，误差略高（最高5.7%），但能可靠检测损伤。低成本无人机系统在检测性能上与商用系统相当，但成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 低成本自主无人机为结构健康监测提供了一种实用、灵活且可扩展的非接触式解决方案，尤其适用于接触式传感不可行的场景。研究结果支持未来部署多架协作无人机以进一步提升检测覆盖范围和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents a non-contact approach for vibration-based structural damage detection using an autonomous and customized cost-effective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Vibration signals are extracted from video recordings through vision-based motion tracking to identify shifts in natural frequencies indicative of structural degradation.
